00:25:19 --> 00:25:24
so the first thing is a projection in

00:25:21 --> 00:25:25
terms of neuroscience is

00:25:23 --> 00:25:27
this long-range connection that goes

00:25:24 --> 00:25:29

00:25:26 --> 00:25:31
and so it's what binds two

00:25:28 --> 00:25:34
parts of our brain together there are

00:25:30 --> 00:25:36
projections long-range connections of

00:25:33 --> 00:25:38
axons these are the outgoing

00:25:35 --> 00:25:40
threads that connect

00:25:37 --> 00:25:41
one part of the brain to another part

00:25:39 --> 00:25:43
here's a there's a projection that

00:25:40 --> 00:25:46
links for example auditory cortex where

00:25:42 --> 00:25:49
we hear things to reward centers where

00:25:45 --> 00:25:50
we can feel where feelings of pleasure

00:25:48 --> 00:25:52

00:25:51 --> 00:25:55
reduced

00:25:52 --> 00:25:57
connectivity along that dimension you

00:25:54 --> 00:25:59
are less able to enjoy music and so

00:25:56 --> 00:26:01
these connections these projections

00:25:58 --> 00:26:02
matter they define

00:26:00 --> 00:26:04

00:26:01 --> 00:26:07

00:26:04 --> 00:26:09

00:26:24 --> 00:26:29

00:26:26 --> 00:26:30

00:26:28 --> 00:26:31

00:26:30 --> 00:26:35
ange too but then there's this other

is

01:03:24 --> 01:03:29
it's very difficult and then even once

01:03:25 --> 01:03:31
you get in it's hard because all brain

01:03:28 --> 01:03:32
cells are electrical all neurons are

01:03:30 --> 01:03:35
lectrically activated and so if you

01:03:31 --> 01:03:38
wanted to use electricity

01:03:34 --> 01:03:40
as what you were putting in

01:03:37 --> 01:03:42
you won't have any specificity at all if

01:03:39 --> 01:03:44
you have an electrode a wire and you put

01:03:41 --> 01:03:46
it in the brain and you send current

01:03:43 --> 01:03:48
hrough it all

01:03:45 --> 01:03:50
the cells near the electrode will be

01:03:47 --> 01:03:54
stimulated it's like trying to control

01:03:49 --> 01:03:56
fish by spraying them with water

01:03:53 --> 01:03:56
yeah right because there's already a lot

of

01:03:55 --> 01:04:00
electricity going around anyway and

you're adding more but there's no

uh specificity even among the different

kinds of cells either because all around

01:04:03 --> 01:04:07
the wire that you've put in they're

01:04:05 --> 01:04:09
going to be so many different cells

01:04:06 --> 01:04:11
doing totally different things many of

01:04:08 --> 01:04:13
them in opposition to each other we know

01:04:10 --> 01:04:15
that's one way the brain is set up there

01:04:12 --> 01:04:17
are parts of the brain that

01:04:14 --> 01:04:18
where neurons side by side are doing

01:04:16 --> 01:04:21
completely different things and maybe

01:04:17 --> 01:04:22

activity with any kind of specificity

01:04:23 --> 01:04:28
well

01:04:24 --> 01:04:29
what you do is use what we found is what

you can do is

01:04:28 --> 01:04:34
make

01:04:30 --> 01:04:35
some cells responsive to light now

01:04:33 --> 01:04:37
normally

01:04:34 --> 01:04:39
no cells deep in the in the brain really

01:04:36 --> 01:04:41
respond to light they're not built for

01:04:38 --> 01:04:43
that there's no reason for them to

01:04:40 --> 01:04:46
respond to light in there

01:04:42 --> 01:04:47
which is a great situation to start with

01:04:45 --> 01:04:50
because any light sensitivity you can

01:04:46 --> 01:04:52
provide to some cells will be a huge

01:04:49 --> 01:04:53
signal above the noise

01:04:51 --> 01:04:56
and so that's that's what we do with

01:04:52 --> 01:04:59
optogenetics we take

01:04:55 --> 01:05:02
genes bits of dna from

01:04:58 --> 01:05:05
icrobes single-celled organisms

01:05:02 --> 01:05:08
and these single-celled organisms like

01:05:04 --> 01:05:10
algae they

01:05:07 --> 01:05:12
make uh little proteins that sit in the

01:05:09 --> 01:05:15
surface of their cells

01:05:11 --> 01:05:16
that receive light capture a photon of

01:05:14 --> 01:05:17
light

01:05:15 --> 01:05:20
and open up a little hole in the

01:05:16 --> 01:05:22
membrane of the cell and let charged

01:05:19 --> 01:05:25
particles ions like sodium and potassium

01:05:21 --> 01:05:28
flow across the membrane of the cell

01:05:24 --> 01:05:29
and that they these algae and bacteria

01:05:27 --> 01:05:31
they do this for their own reasons

01:05:28 --> 01:05:32
because that helps them move it helps

01:05:30 --> 01:05:35
them

01:05:31 --> 01:05:36
ake and use energy

01:05:34 --> 01:05:39
but that's a beautiful thing for

01:05:35 --> 01:05:41
neuroscience because movement of ions

01:05:38 --> 01:05:43
charged particles across the membrane of

01:05:40 --> 01:05:45
the cell is exactly the kind of

01:05:42 --> 01:05:46
electricity that neurons work with so if

01:05:44 --> 01:05:48
we can take this

01:05:45 --> 01:05:49
bit of dna

01:05:47 --> 01:05:52
that encodes this beautiful protein that

01:05:48 --> 01:05:54
urns light into electricity from algae

01:05:51 --> 01:05:56
and if we can put it into

01:05:53 --> 01:05:59
some neurons but not other neurons which

01:05:55 --> 01:06:00
we can do using genetic tricks

01:05:58 --> 01:06:02
then you've got a situation then you can

01:05:59 --> 01:06:03
shine on the light

and only

01:06:02 --> 01:06:06
the cells that have the gene and that

01:06:04 --> 01:06:09

01:06:05 --> 01:06:10
will be the initial direct cells that

01:06:08 --> 01:06:12
are activated by the light and so that's

01:06:09 --> 01:06:14
the essence of optogenetics is the

01:06:11 --> 01:06:15
ability to do that we get that initial

01:06:13 --> 01:06:17
specificity that you could never get
